(155)

Appendix B
Derivation of kinematic flow from intersection theory

In this appendix, we derive closed form formulas for the intersection matrix (appendix
B.1
) and the matrix elements of differential equation (appendix
B.2
and
B.3
).

B.1
Intersection matrix

In this appendix, we illustrate how to compute the intersection matrix (
125
) from the localization formula (
123
).
Recalling the definition of our basis forms and the fact that our basis of FRW- and dual-forms contain one cut (i.e.,
\text{Res}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}}[\phi_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}]\propto|\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\delta_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}
),

\displaystyle\langle\check{\phi}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\phi_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}|}}\circ\cdots\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}}\circ\text{Res}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}}[\mathrm{dlog}\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}]\,,

(156)

\displaystyle=|\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\delta_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}|}}\circ\cdots\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{g}}]\,,

(157)

where
|\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}|
is the number of tubings that belong to this cut; it will always cancel in the matrix elements of the DEQ.
Then, recalling that
\Omega_{\mathfrak{g}}=\bigwedge_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}}\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}
,

\displaystyle\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}

\displaystyle=\bigwedge_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}}}\mathrm{dlog}\frac{x_{v}}{x_{\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}}}}\bigg|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}=\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\mathrm{sig}(v,V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v)\bigwedge_{u\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v}\mathrm{dlog}x_{u}\bigg|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}\,,

(158)

and the definition of
\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}}
(equation (
124
)) yields

\displaystyle\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{v,w\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\frac{\alpha_{v}\mathrm{sig}(v,V_{\mathfrak{l}}{\setminus}v)\mathrm{sig}(w,V_{\mathfrak{l}}{\setminus}w)}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\overset{\delta_{vw}}{\overbrace{\underset{u\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v}{\bigcirc}\text{Res}_{x_{v}=0}\left.{\bigwedge_{y\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus y}}\mathrm{dlog}x_{y}\right|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}}}

(159)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\,.

Therefore,

\displaystyle C_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}:=\langle\check{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\varphi_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\rangle=|\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\delta_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\prod_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}]=|\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\delta_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\prod_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}}\frac{\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\,.

(160)

B.2
The DEQ: diagonal elements

To derive equation (
128
), note that

\displaystyle\text{Res}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}}\left[\omega\wedge\mathrm{dlog}\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{g}}\right]=(-1)^{|V_{\mathfrak{g}}|}|\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}|\;\Omega_{\mathfrak{g}}\wedge\omega|_{\mathcal{C}_{g}}\,,

(161)

\displaystyle\omega|_{\mathcal{C}_{g}}=\sum_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{g}}\left[\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}\,\mathrm{dlog}x_{l}\right]_{f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}:=\sum_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{g}}\omega_{\mathfrak{l}}\,,

(162)

where we recall that
f_{\mathfrak{l}}
only depends (linearly) on the
X_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}
:
f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}x_{l}+f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})
.
Collecting the
x_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}
-dependence and separating the residues while being careful of the sign generated by the wedge product yields

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}|}}\circ\cdots\circ\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{g}}\wedge\omega]\Big|_{\mathcal{C}_{g}}\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}|}\left[\left(\prod_{j\neq i}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{j}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{j}}]\bigg|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}_{j}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}\right)\left(\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{i}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{i}}\wedge\omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{i}}]\bigg|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}\right)\right]\,.\end{aligned}

(163)

The factors in the first brackets above were computed already in (
159
).
To evaluate the remaining factors, note that

\displaystyle\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}\wedge\omega_{\mathfrak{l}}=\underset{(-1)^{|V_{\mathfrak{l}}|-1}}{\underbrace{\mathrm{sig}(\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}},V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}})\mathrm{sig}(V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}},\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}})}}\left(\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}\right)\left(\bigwedge_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\mathrm{dlog}x_{v}\bigg|_{\mathbf{x}+f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}\right)\,.

(164)

Then, since each term of
\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}}
sets all but one
x_{l_{i}\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}
to zero

\displaystyle\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}\wedge\omega_{\mathfrak{l}}]

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{|V_{\mathfrak{l}}|-1}\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{v}\mathrm{sig}(v,V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v)\underset{u\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v}{\bigcirc}\text{Res}_{x_{v}=0}\left.{\bigwedge_{w\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}}\mathrm{dlog}x_{w}\right|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{|V_{\mathfrak{l}}|-1}\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\left.\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{v}\mathrm{sig}(v,V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v)\mathrm{sig}(V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v,v)\mathrm{dlog}x_{v}\right|_{f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})=0,x_{w\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}\setminus v}=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\left(\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{v}\right)\mathrm{dlog}f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})\,.

(165)

Substituting into (
163
), yields

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:AdiagResT}=\left[\prod_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}|}\left(\frac{\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}{\prod_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}\alpha_{l}}\right)\right]\left[\sum_{\mathfrak{l}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}|}\left({\textstyle\sum_{l\in V_{\mathfrak{l}}}}\alpha_{l}\right)\mathrm{dlog}f_{\mathfrak{l}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})\right]\,.

(166)

Combining (
62
) with
\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}
, (
163
) and (
161
) yields (
128
).

B.3
The DEQ: off-diagonal elements

Restoring the sign in (
133
) yields

\displaystyle A_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle=}\frac{(-1)^{|V_{G}|}}{\langle\check{\phi}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}|\phi_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\rangle}\sum_{(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\hskip-10.00002pt\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau^{\prime}}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau\tau^{\prime}}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}|}}{\circ}{\cdots}{\circ}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}_{1}}\left[\mathrm{dlog}B_{\tau\setminus\tau^{\prime}}{\wedge}\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{g}}{\wedge}\omega\right]\bigg|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\,,

(167)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle=}\frac{|\mathcal{C}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}|}{\langle\check{\phi}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}|\phi_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\rangle}\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}|}}{\circ}{\cdots}{\circ}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}_{1}}\left[\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{g}}{\wedge}\omega{\wedge}\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\right]\bigg|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\,,

where
\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau\tau^{\prime}}
compares the relative ordering of the tubes for a pair
(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}
,
and
\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}
is the same for all
(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}
.
Explicitly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau^{\prime}}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau\tau^{\prime}}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{sig}(\langle\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{g}}\rangle_{\tau})\mathrm{sig}(\langle\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\rangle_{\tau},\langle\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{g}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\rangle_{\tau})\,,

(168)

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle:=(-1)^{|\tau^{\prime}|}\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}}\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau^{\prime}}\mathrm{sgn}_{\tau\tau^{\prime}}\,.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{dlog}B_{\tau\setminus\tau^{\prime}}\bigg|_{B_{t\in\tau^{\prime}}=0}\,,

(169)

is also the same for all pairs
(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}
.
While we do not provide an explicit expression for this intermediate object, it has following properties that we be used later

\displaystyle\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}|_{x_{i\in V_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}}=0}

\displaystyle=f_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})\,,

\displaystyle\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}|_{x_{i\in V_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}}=0}

\displaystyle=f_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})\,.

(170)

We have also anti-commuted
\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}
to the end of the wedge product which produces a sign
(-1)^{|V_{G}|-|\tau^{\prime}|}
and exposes the canonical forms
\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}}
on which
\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}
acts.

Then, performing the
\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}
yields
20
20

20

It is possible that
\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}
and
\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}
may not be adjacent in
\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}
when ordered by their minimal vertex (as dictated in the definition of
\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{g}}
), for a given labeling.
In such cases, another sign could be generated

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}_{\uparrow\downarrow}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{sig}(\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}},{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}})\mathrm{sig}(\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}},{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\uparrow}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\uparrow}},V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}},V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}},{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}\setminus\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}})\,,

(171)

\displaystyle V_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\uparrow}}

\displaystyle=(V_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}^{\prime\uparrow}},\dots,V_{\mathfrak{l}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}|}}^{\prime\uparrow}})\,,\qquad\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\uparrow}}=(\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}^{\prime\uparrow}},\dots,\overline{V}_{\mathfrak{l}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}|}}^{\prime\uparrow}})\,.

Here, one exchanges
\uparrow
with
\downarrow
in the obvious way to get
V_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}
and
\overline{V}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}
.
In all checked cases,
\mathrm{sgn}_{\uparrow\downarrow}=1
.
Yet, we have not been able to show this in full generality from the above formula.

\displaystyle A_{\mathfrak{g}{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle=}\frac{\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}{\prod_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\in(\underline{\mathfrak{l}}\cup\mathbb{L}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}^{\downarrow})}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}]}\text{Res}_{\mathbb{L}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}^{\downarrow}}{\circ}\text{Res}_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}}\bigg[\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}{\wedge}\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}{\wedge}\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}{\wedge}\omega{\wedge}\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\bigg]\!\bigg|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\!\!\!,

(172)

where

\displaystyle\text{Res}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}

\displaystyle:=\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}|}^{\prime\downarrow}}\circ\cdots\circ\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}^{\prime\downarrow}}\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}

\displaystyle:=\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{1}^{\prime\downarrow}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime\downarrow}_{|\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}|}}\,.

(173)

For this to be non-zero,
\omega
must donate some
\mathrm{dlog}x_{\bullet}
’s to
\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}
.
Anti-commuting the parts of
\omega
that survive the restriction of the wedge product to the cut,
\omega_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}=\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}}\alpha_{v}\mathrm{dlog}x_{v}
and
\omega_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}=\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}}\alpha_{v}\mathrm{dlog}x_{v}
,
behind
\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}
at the cost of another sign

\displaystyle A_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle{=}\frac{\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}(-1)^{\sum_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}(|V_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}|-1)}(-1)^{|V_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}|-1}}{\prod_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\in(\underline{\mathfrak{l}}\cup\mathbb{L}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}^{\downarrow})}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}]}

(174)

\displaystyle\times\text{Res}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}\circ\text{Res}_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}}\bigg[\left(\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}\wedge\omega_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}+(-1)^{|V_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}|-1}\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}\wedge\omega_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}\right)\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\downarrow}}\wedge\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}\,.

Next, note that

\displaystyle\text{Res}_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}}[\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}\wedge\omega_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{L}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\downarrow}}\wedge\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}]|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}

(175)

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{sig}(V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}},V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}})\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}]\,\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}]\,\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\downarrow}}\wedge\underset{\mathrm{dlog}f_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})}{\underbrace{\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}\setminus\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}},x_{l\in V_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}=0}}}}\,,

\displaystyle\text{Res}_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}}[\tilde{\Omega}_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}\wedge\omega_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}\wedge\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\downarrow}}\wedge\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}\setminus\mathfrak{g}}]|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{sig}(V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}},V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}})(-1)^{|V_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}|-1}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}]\,\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}]\,\tilde{\Omega}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\downarrow}}\wedge\underset{\mathrm{dlog}f_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})}{\underbrace{\mathrm{dlog}B_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}\setminus\mathfrak{g}}|_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{g}},x_{l\in V_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}=0}}}}\,.

Then, equation (
174
) becomes

\displaystyle A_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle=}\mathfrak{s}(\mathfrak{g},{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}})\frac{\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}]\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}]}{\text{Res}_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}}[\Omega_{\underline{\mathfrak{l}}}]}\;\overset{1}{\overbrace{\frac{\text{Res}_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}\left[\Omega_{\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\uparrow}}\right]}{\prod_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}_{{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}}^{\downarrow}}\text{Res}_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}]}}}\;\mathrm{dlog}\frac{f_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})}{f_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})}\,,

(176)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{s}(\mathfrak{g},{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}})

\displaystyle:=-\mathrm{sig}(V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}},V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}})\mathrm{sgn}_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}(-1)^{\sum_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}_{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}^{\downarrow}}(|V_{\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}}|-1)+(|V_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}|-1)}\,.

One can show that the sign above simplifies to

\displaystyle\mathfrak{s}(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}\phantom{-}1&\text{if oriented edges connecting $\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}$ to $\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}$ point away from $\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}$}\\
-1&\text{if oriented edges connecting $\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}$ to $\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}$ point towards $\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}$}\end{cases}\,.

(177)

Finally, taking the remaining residues yields

\displaystyle A_{\mathfrak{g}\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{s}(\mathfrak{g},{\mathfrak{g}^{\prime}})\frac{\Big(\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}}\alpha_{v}\Big)\Big(\sum_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}}\alpha_{v}\Big)}{\prod_{v\in V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\uparrow}}\cup V_{\mathfrak{l}_{\downarrow}}}\alpha_{v}}\mathrm{dlog}\frac{f_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\uparrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})}{f_{{\mathfrak{l}}_{\downarrow}}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})}\,.

(178)
